In high-stakes financial settings, this leads to unreliable tool routing, noisy retrieval, and hallucination-prone reasoning. We present FinAcumen, a financial reasoning agent framework centered on selective experience memory for tool-augmented multimodal reasoning. FinAcumen accumulates financially grounded reasoning experience from prior trajectories, distilling successful strategies and failure-derived cautionary rules into a persistent memory bank. During inference, retrieved experiences condition reasoning only when semantic relevance exceeds a calibrated threshold, while irrelevant memory is explicitly suppressed through a fallback mechanism. A deterministic financial tool environment further grounds numerical computation, retrieval, visual decoding, and answer verification.Across four financial multimodal reasoning benchmarks, FinAcumen consistently improves a frozen 8B vision-language model over finance-specialized models and approaches leading proprietary general-purpose models. Further analysis shows that selective experience activation improves reasoning reliability under retrieval uncertainty. Our code is anonymously available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/FinAcumen

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Domains: Reusing Web Skills via Transferable Interaction Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17645)
*Shiqi He,Yue Cui,Feijie Wu,Xinyu Ma,Jiaheng Lu,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillMigrator是一个通过布局结构匹配而非元素引用实现跨站点Web技能迁移的智能体，在保持成功率的同时将LLM动作数减少8-10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Web智能体依赖低层级原子操作工具调用，导致任务序列过长、LLM推理开销大；而现有技能库主要依赖指令相似性或粗粒度站点元数据触发，在未见站点上复用率低，未能充分减少步骤和token消耗。

Method: SkillMigrator将每个诱导技能存储为可迁移交互模式（TIP），包含技能本身及其归纳时刻的快照结构草图；测试时通过布局相似性检索TIP，并在实时页面上对其引用进行锚定；其余部分采用标准的无障碍快照观察和固定工具调用流程。

Result: 在WebArena和Mind2Web上，SkillMigrator在匹配的成功率下，将成功轨迹上的平均LLM动作数减少了8-10%。

Conclusion: 通过布局结构匹配而非元素引用来迁移Web技能是有效的，能够显著降低LLM调用次数而不牺牲任务成功率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) web agents are usually deployed as tool callers: each turn, the model reads a fresh page observation and emits one structured tool action. When every action is a low-level primitive, horizons grow quickly and so do policy-facing LLM completions, dominating latency and cost on benchmarks such as Mind2Web and WebArena. Recent systems therefore wrap repeated interaction fragments as web skills: callable tools built from successful trajectories or induced programs, so one call can replace several primitives. However, prior skill libraries are still triggered mainly by instruction similarity or coarse site metadata, which yields low skill reuse on held-out sites and leaves much of the potential step and token reduction on the table.
  We present SkillMigrator, an agent that learns reusable web skills and transfers them across sites by matching layout structure rather than specific element references. Each induced skill is stored as a transferable interaction pattern (TIP): the skill paired with a structural sketch of the snapshot at induction time. At test time, SkillMigrator retrieves TIPs by layout similarity and grounds their references on the live page. The rest of the stack is standard: accessibility-snapshot observations with stable references, and fixed tool calling over primitives plus skill invocations. Compared with the state-of-the-art approaches, SkillMigrator reduces the average LLM-action count on successful trajectories by 8-10% across both WebArena and Mind2Web at matched success rate.

</details>


### [76] [From Brewing to Resolution: Tracing the Internal Lifecycle of Code Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17648)
*Siyue Chen,Yifu Guo,Yuquan Lu,Zishan Xu,Jiaye Lin,Jianbo Lin,Siyu Zhang,Cheng Yang,Junxin Li,Yujia Li,Yu Huo,Ruixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种双诊断框架（层间线性探针+上下文剥离解码），揭示了LLM在代码推理中的“酝酿”生命周期：模型先线性可恢复地“酝酿”答案，随后分化为四种结局（已解决、过度处理、误解决、未解决）。在16个模型和6个任务族上，仅41.5%被正确解决，且功能调用深度从1增至3时解决率从61.1%骤降至2.5%。酝酿支架在架构间稳定（归一化酝酿时长24-42%），而解决成功率随能力、规模和训练变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率指标无法解释LLM为何能处理变量追踪任务却在语义等价的循环任务上失败，因此需要深入理解模型内部代码推理的生命周期及其失败模式。

Method: 提出双诊断框架：层间线性探针（layer-wise linear probing）结合上下文剥离解码（Context-Stripped Decoding, CSD），应用于Qwen、Llama、DeepSeek架构的16个模型及6个代码推理任务族。

Result: 所有四种结局在每个任务族中均大量存在，总体解决率仅41.5%，多个任务低于30%。功能调用深度从1增至3时解决率从61.1%暴跌至2.5%。归一化酝酿时长在16个模型中稳定在24-42%区间。

Conclusion: 酝酿支架是测试的decoder-only Transformer系列中稳定的经验规律，而解决成功率与模型能力、规模和训练协变。表面评估无法捕捉根本不同的失败模式。

Abstract: Standard accuracy metrics cannot explain why LLMs handle variable tracking but fail on semantically equivalent loops. We study an internal lifecycle of code reasoning in which models first brew the answer, making it linearly recoverable many layers before it becomes self-decodable, and then diverge into one of four resolution outcomes: Resolved, Overprocessed, Misresolved, or Unresolved. Understanding this lifecycle matters because similar task accuracies can mask fundamentally different failure modes that surface-level evaluation cannot detect. We introduce a dual diagnostic framework pairing layer-wise linear probing with Context-Stripped Decoding (CSD) and apply it to six code-reasoning task families across 16 models spanning Qwen, Llama, and DeepSeek architectures. All four outcomes carry substantial mass in every task family: overall Resolved is only 41.5%, with multiple tasks below 30%. Controlled sweeps over structure, depth, and operators expose task-specific failure bottlenecks: Function Call Resolved plunges from 61.1% to 2.5% as call depth increases from one to three. Across architectures and scales, the brewing scaffold remains stable, with normalized brewing duration 24-42% across all 16 models, while resolution success varies with capability. This indicates that the scaffold is a stable empirical regularity across the tested decoder-only Transformer families, whereas resolution success covaries with capability, scale, and training. Code: https://github.com/euyis1019/llm-brewing

</details>


### [77] [Using Cognitive Models to Improve Language Model Simulation of Human Persuasion Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17657)
*Zirui Cheng,Zeyu Shen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Peter Henderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Equation-to-Behavior方法，通过数学认知模型指导大语言模型模拟人类决策中的信念更新偏差（如贝叶斯更新、动机推理等），并在说服游戏中验证。大模型可通过提示工程近似数学模型，小模型则需强化学习（Equation-to-Behavior RL）将信念错误降低26.5%。基于此构建多样化训练环境，可使信念改变提升2.5%-12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全评估和训练常使用模拟人类，但现有模拟未能覆盖人类决策中广泛的行为异质性（如贝叶斯更新与动机推理等偏差）。作者认为认知科学和经济学中的人类决策数学模型可以作为工具来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出Equation-to-Behavior Prompting方法，利用数学认知模型引导LLM模拟人类决策行为。在基于法律决策的说服游戏中进行评估，测试贝叶斯更新、仿射畸变、动机更新和Grether α-β模型。对大模型使用提示工程，对小模型使用强化学习（Equation-to-Behavior RL）使其遵守数学规则。

Result: 大模型能通过提示工程近似方程化模型，小模型则失败；但使用强化学习训练的小模型在分布外参数化下信念错误降低26.5%。训练小模型考虑多种决策者类型，平均信念改变比仅贝叶斯训练提升2.5%-12%，即使在说服GPT-5-mini时也有效。

Conclusion: 该工作可改进现实场景中用于训练和评估的人类模拟，也为研究更复杂的人类决策数学模型提供了新工具。

Abstract: People make decisions differently in strategic interactions. Some update beliefs like a Bayesian; others exhibit biases like motivated reasoning. Although creators of large language models use simulated humans for safety evaluations and training, they often fail to cover this breadth of human behavior. We argue that cognitive science and economics provide a convenient tool for doing so, making use of mathematical models of human decision-making. We propose an approach that we call Equation-to-Behavior Prompting for guiding large language models to match cognitive models, and evaluate this approach on persuasion games based on legal decision-making. We find that large models can approximate equation-based specifications -- Bayesian updating, affine distortion, motivated updating, and Grether's $α$-$β$ model -- using prompting, but small models fail to do so. However, training small models with reinforcement learning to adhere to mathematical rules, Equation-to-Behavior RL, reduces belief error by 26.5% in out-of-distribution parameterizations. We show that these simulations can help create diverse training environments; training small models to consider different kinds of decision-makers improves average belief change by 2.5%--12% over Bayesian-only training, even when persuading GPT-5-mini. Our work could improve human simulations for training and evaluation in increasingly realistic settings, and could also enable novel research into more complicated mathematical models of human decision-making.

</details>


### [78] [FllumaOne: A Code-Native Multimodal CAD Dataset with Executable Programs and Kernel-Validated Feature Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17696)
*Jizong Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FllumaOne是一个代码原生多模态CAD数据集，包含10万个由可执行Python程序生成的模型，每个样本对齐了程序、特征树、STEP几何、点云、描述、元数据和八视角渲染图。基于Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B LoRA的基线模型在保留测试集上达到了99.98%的Python语法有效性和99.14%的STEP导出有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可编辑CAD数据集缺乏同时包含建模操作、参数、特征依赖关系和经过验证的几何信息的数据，难以支撑可编辑CAD研究，因此需要构建一个代码原生的多模态CAD数据集。

Method: 在Flluma（基于Qt/C++和OpenCASCADE的CAD系统）中通过可执行的Python程序生成模型，每个样本包含程序、结构化特征树、训练导向的中间表示、STEP几何、表面点云、自然语言描述、元数据和八个规范视角的可见边渲染图。数据集经过内核几何、实体有效性和导出检查后才保留样本。

Result: FllumaOne-100K包含10万个样本，横跨四个模板级复杂度区间。基线模型在1万样本测试集上：Python语法有效性99.98%，Flluma构建成功率99.97%，STEP导出有效性99.14%，表面点云平均归一化Chamfer距离0.002124。

Conclusion: 该数据集可支持条件化CAD重建、可执行程序合成、特征树预测、B-Rep分析、检索、设计补全和可编辑逆向工程等多种任务。

Abstract: Parametric computer-aided design records both final geometry and the ordered construction history that determines how a part can be edited. Datasets for editable CAD research should therefore expose modeling operations, parameters, and feature dependencies together with validated geometry. We introduce FllumaOne, a code-native multimodal CAD dataset whose models are generated by executable Python programs in Flluma, a Qt/C++ OpenCASCADE-based CAD system. Each sample aligns its program with a structured feature tree, a training-oriented intermediate representation, STEP geometry, a surface point cloud, natural-language descriptions, metadata, and eight canonical visible-edge renderings.
  The primary release, FllumaOne-100K, contains 100,000 accepted samples across four template-level complexity regimes. Programs are executed and retained only after kernel geometry, solid validity, and export checks; release reports also record modality completeness and split-level duplicate tests. A Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B LoRA baseline trained on 80,000 samples achieves 99.98% Python syntax validity, 99.97% Flluma build success, and 99.14% STEP-export validity on the held-out 10,000-sample test split. For the 9,909 predictions converted to surface point clouds, the mean normalized Chamfer Distance is 0.002124. The dataset supports conditioned CAD reconstruction, executable program synthesis, feature-tree prediction, B-Rep analysis, retrieval, design completion, and editable reverse engineering.

</details>


### [79] [EComAgentBench: Benchmarking Shopping Agents on Long-Horizon Tasks with Distributed Hidden Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17698)
*Zeyao Du,Tong Li,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EComAgentBench 是一个包含662个任务的基准测试，用于评估LLM驱动的购物代理在真实电商场景中的表现。任务将用户需求分散在可见查询、工具限制的配置文件和脚本化澄清中，要求代理在100次工具调用内发现隐藏意图并验证候选产品。最强模型仅达57.1%准确率，且需求来源越隐蔽，满足度越低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未能捕捉真实购物场景中需求的呈现方式（隐式表达、配置文件中记录、仅当提出正确问题时才揭示），且仅评估最终选择，无法解释代理遗漏了哪个需求。

Method: 基于真实亚马逊产品和评论构建662个任务，将需求分散在可见查询、工具门控的配置文件和脚本化澄清三个来源；使用带类型和来源标签的评分标准逐项评估任务，自动构建但每个答案在代码中固定且每个样本都经过验证。

Result: 对7个模型的评估显示，最强模型总体准确率仅57.1%，评分满意度从可见来源到隐藏来源逐级下降。

Conclusion: EComAgentBench可作为可复现的基础，推动购物代理从单查询搜索向长周期可靠辅助方向发展。

Abstract: As LLM-based shopping agents enter production, existing benchmarks fail to capture how a shopper's requirements arrive: stated implicitly in the query, recorded in a profile, or revealed only when the right question is asked. Benchmarks that expose full intent upfront and grade only the final choice can neither pose this long-horizon challenge nor explain which requirement an agent missed. To address this gap, we introduce EComAgentBench, a benchmark of 662 tasks grounded in real Amazon products and reviews. Each task scatters these requirements across a visible query, a tool-gated profile, and scripted clarification; an agent must uncover hidden intent, verify candidates against attributes and review evidence, and commit to a single product within 100 tool calls. Moreover, typed, source-tagged rubrics grade every task, attributing each failure to a requirement and its source. Construction is automated yet reliable, with every answer fixed in code before any text is generated and every sample validated. Our evaluation of seven models reveals that even the strongest attains only 57.1% overall accuracy, and rubric satisfaction degrades from visible to hidden sources. Overall, we believe EComAgentBench will serve as a reproducible foundation for moving shopping agents from single-query search toward dependable assistance over long horizons.

</details>


### [80] [LongWebBench: Evaluating Structural and Functional Webpage Generation in Long-Horizon Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17727)
*Yi Zhao,Zhen Yang,Mengpan Chen,Mingde Xu,Shanghui Gong,Xijun Liu,Jibing Gong,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongWebBench是一个用于评估长篇幅网页生成的基准，包含490个真实长网页的结构评估和507个功能交互任务。实验表明，随着网页长度增加，结构保真度下降，视觉上合理的生成往往无法支持可执行的多步交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在网页生成方面的评估主要集中于短、单屏、静态网页，缺乏对长篇幅、多交互网页的评估能力。

Method: 构建包含490个真实长网页（结构评估）和507个目标导向交互任务（功能评估）的基准LongWebBench；采用多维VLM指标评估长程结构连贯性，以及DOM增强的智能体流水线进行端到端功能验证。

Result: 结构保真度随网页长度增加而下降；视觉上合理的生成往往无法支持可执行的多步交互。

Conclusion: 长网页生成评估需要超越视觉相似性，应将可执行交互作为核心标准。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising progress in generating webpages from visual inputs, yet existing evaluations mainly focus on short, single-screen, and largely static webpages. We introduce LongWebBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon webpage generation from both structural and functional perspectives. LongWebBench contains 490 real-world long webpages for structural fidelity evaluation and 507 goal-oriented interaction tasks over 129 webpages for functional evaluation. It employs two complementary protocols: a multi-dimensional VLM-based metric for assessing long-range structural coherence, and a DOM-augmented agent-based pipeline for end-to-end functional verification. We further examine the automatic evaluation protocols through human agreement analysis. Experiments with state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary VLMs under single-image and multi-image settings reveal that structural fidelity degrades as webpage length increases, while visually plausible generations often fail to support executable multi-step interactions. These results highlight the need to evaluate long webpage generation beyond visual similarity, with executable interaction as a core criterion. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/zheny2751-dotcom/LongWebBench.

</details>


### [81] [Shattering the Autoregressive Curse: Dynamic Epistemic Entropy Orchestrated Erasable Reinforcement Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17735)
*Ziliang Wang,Kang An,Faqiang Qian,Jialu Cai,Cijun Ouyang,Yuhang Wang,Qibing Ren,Yichao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动态 epistemic 熵编排可擦除强化学习（E³RL），通过将模型内生的局部自回归交叉熵作为认知不确定性指标，引入分段级自适应动态阈值和优势分配，使模型能够精准切除局部逻辑缺陷并复用历史 KV 缓存流，从而打破长序列推理中的自回归诅咒，在 AIME 等数学推理基准上，4B 和 8B 模型分别超越此前 SOTA 5.349% 和 6.514%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽扩展了LLM的认知边界，但在长程逻辑推理中易受自回归诅咒影响：早期微小认知扰动沿MDP流不可逆传播，引发级联失败导致推理轨迹崩溃。

Method: E³RL：将模型内生的局部自回归交叉熵作为认知不确定性坐标（无需外部信号）；引入分段级自适应动态阈值和优势分配；可精准切除局部逻辑缺陷并复用历史KV缓存流。

Result: 在DeepMath-103k上训练，AIME基准上4B模型提升5.349%，8B模型提升6.514%，均超越此前SOTA；保持线性内存开销，提升长序列探索效率和样本效率。

Conclusion: E³RL打破了长序列推理中的自回归诅咒，为下一代自愈型AGI奠定了理论和系统级基础。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning (RL) has expanded the cognitive boundaries of large language models (LLMs), it often remains vulnerable to the autoregressive curse in long-horizon logical reasoning: small epistemic perturbations introduced early in generation can propagate irreversibly along the Markov decision process flow, triggering cascading failures that drive the reasoning trajectory toward collapse. To overcome this autoregressive cascade, in which a single early mistake can compromise all subsequent reasoning steps, we propose dynamic epistemic entropy orchestrated erasable reinforcement learning ($\text{E}^3\text{RL}$). $\text{E}^3\text{RL}$ eliminates reliance on external signals by grounding the model's endogenous local autoregressive cross-entropy as an intrinsic coordinate of epistemic uncertainty. By introducing segment-level adaptive dynamic thresholds and advantage allocation, $\text{E}^3\text{RL}$ enables the model to precisely excise localized logical defects while reusing historical key-value (KV) cache streams, thereby endowing the reasoning process with a self-healing capability. We train $\text{E}^3\text{RL}$ on the DeepMath-103k dataset. Experimental results show that $\text{E}^3\text{RL}$ reshapes the exploration efficiency of long-sequence reasoning and improves sample efficiency while maintaining linear memory overhead. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks such as AIME, $\text{E}^3\text{RL}$ achieves substantial performance gains, with the 4B and 8B parameter models surpassing previous state-of-the-art (SOTA) results by 5.349\% and 6.514\%, respectively. These findings suggest that $\text{E}^3\text{RL}$ shatters the autoregressive curse in long-sequence reasoning and establishes a theoretical and systems-level foundation for the next generation of self-healing artificial general intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [82] [DecoSearch: Complexity-Aware Routing and Plan-Level Repair for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17821)
*Esteban Schafir,Xu Zheng,Hojat Allah Salehi,Zhuomin Chen,Mo Sha,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecoSearch是一种无需训练的框架，通过动态路由（直接生成或DAG分解）处理NL-to-SQL任务，结合Schema选择、LLM判断、RAG增强和拓扑重构，在BIRD和Spider上超越所有无训练基线，且大幅降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在复杂、需要多步数据感知推理的NL-to-SQL查询上表现不佳。

Method: 1) Schema Selector裁剪相关表/列；2) LLM Judger判断是否需要分解；3) 复杂问题拆解为DAG原子子问题，每个子问题独立生成SQL；4) RAG组件用相似训练示例增强分解器；5) Topology Refiner在执行失败时重构推理计划。

Result: BIRD上70.53%执行准确率，Spider上88.31%，使用DeepSeek骨干，超越所有无训练基线，token消耗比竞品低一个数量级。

Conclusion: DecoSearch作为模型无关的包装器，无需修改管道即可一致提升微调后的SQL生成骨干模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in translating natural language to SQL, yet existing methods still falter on complex queries requiring multi-step, data-aware reasoning. We introduce DecoSearch, a training-free framework that addresses this by routing each query to the appropriate level of reasoning effort. A lightweight Schema Selector first prunes the full database schema to the relevant tables and columns. An LLM Judger then decides whether the question requires decomposition: straightforward questions follow a direct generation path and complex ones are escalated to a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) of atomic sub-questions, each solved by a targeted SQL generation step. A RAG component grounds the decomposer with semantically similar training examples, and a Topology Refiner restructures the reasoning plan when execution failures signal a flawed decomposition rather than a fixable SQL error. DecoSearch achieves 70.53% execution accuracy on BIRD and 88.31% on Spider with a DeepSeek backbone, surpassing all training-free baselines while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens than competing methods. It also functions as a model-agnostic wrapper, consistently improving fine-tuned SQL generation backbones without any modification to the pipeline.

</details>


### [83] [WallZero: Mastering the Game of WallGo with Strategic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17847)
*Hsing-Yu Chen,Jérôme Arjonilla,I-Chen Wu,Ti-Rong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WallZero，一个基于AlphaZero的WallGo游戏智能体，通过定制化动作和特征设计，能够击败职业围棋选手，并分析了游戏平衡性与关键策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WallGo是一款新兴的战略棋盘游戏，虽因Netflix剧集而流行，但缺乏深入的研究和强大的AI智能体。

Method: 基于AlphaZero框架，针对WallGo设计了定制化的动作空间和特征表示，用于训练双人对抗的强化学习智能体。

Result: WallZero击败了两名参与研究的职业围棋选手，平均每局获得1.98倍于对手的领地；发现Netflix剧集中使用的开局方式能带来更平衡的游戏。

Conclusion: WallZero展示了强于人类专家的水平，同时通过分析揭示了WallGo的游戏平衡性和有效策略。

Abstract: WallGo is a recently introduced strategic board game popularized by the 2025 Netflix series The Devil's Plan. Although played on a small 7 x 7 board, its combination of stone movement and wall placement yields high game-tree complexity and intricate strategic interactions. Despite its growing popularity, WallGo remains underexplored. This paper presents WallZero, an AlphaZero-based agent for the two-player WallGo setting. We introduce tailored action and feature designs to improve playing performance significantly. In the evaluation, WallZero defeats two professional Go players who participated in this study, securing on average 1.98x more territory per game. Beyond its strength, we use WallZero to assess game fairness and identify key strategies for mastering WallGo. Interestingly, our results show that the opening used in the Netflix series yields a more balanced game. Our code is available at https://rlg.iis.sinica.edu.tw/papers/wallzero.

</details>


### [84] [A homotopy-type-theoretic generalization of neurosymbolic inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17851)
*Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用同伦类型论中的类型替代集合论，将神经符号系统中通用的信念加权求和泛函转化为信念加权同伦基数，从而保留对称性和证明计数信息。该方法能自然暴露推理捷径问题，并通过单一模型在对称群上平均化得到捷径感知的概念后验闭式解，在MNIST基准上相比集成方法具有更好的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经符号系统基于集合论构建信念加权求和泛函，但集合会遗忘两类关键信息：(1) σ-结构在理论对称性下的等价性；(2) 一个查询有多少个不同的证明。这导致系统无法正确处理对称性，进而产生推理捷径问题。

Method: 用同伦类型论中的类型替换集合论，将传统的信念加权求和泛函重新形式化为信念加权同伦基数——一种按对称性倒数计数对象的规模度量。证明了当对称性平凡时该框架退化为经典泛函的保守性定理，并表明框架暴露的对称性正是推理捷径背后的对称性。

Result: 捷径感知的概念后验是混淆集单纯形上唯一的对称不变点，可通过单一模型在对称群上平均化得到闭式解。在MNIST推理捷径基准上，该单一模型包装器相比多样性训练的集成具有更好的校准性能，且标签准确率和可识别概念不变。

Conclusion: 同伦类型论为神经符号系统提供了保留对称性和证明信息的理论基础，能够自然解决推理捷径问题，无需集成或复杂密度估计，仅需单一模型在对称群上平均化即可得到理论最优的捷径感知后验。

Abstract: A wide range of neurosymbolic (NeSy) systems compute one functional: a belief-weighted sum of a logical quantity over a space of $σ$-structures, of which weighted model counting, fuzzy logic, and probabilistic logic are special cases. This account is built on sets, and a set deliberately forgets two things that are important for NeSy: when two $σ$-structures are the same up to a symmetry of the theory, and how many distinct proofs witness a query. Replacing the underlying sets by types, in the sense of homotopy type theory, preserves this information, and turns this functional into a belief-weighted homotopy cardinality, a notion of size that counts each object in inverse proportion to its symmetries. We develop the framework from scratch for NeSy systems, prove a conservativity theorem that recovers the classical functional when symmetries are trivial, and show that the symmetry our framework exposes is exactly the one behind reasoning shortcuts. The payoff is concrete: the shortcut-aware concept posterior that recent methods reach by ensembling or expressive density estimation is the only symmetry-invariant point of the confusion-set simplex, computable in closed form by averaging a single model over the symmetry group. On MNIST reasoning-shortcut benchmarks this single-model wrapper is better calibrated than a diversity-trained ensemble, while leaving label accuracy and identifiable concepts untouched. Code is freely available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/hott-nesy.

</details>


### [85] [FlowRAG: Synergizing Explicit Reasoning via Frequency-Aware Multi-Granularity Graph Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17856)
*Bihao Zhan,Zongsheng Cao,Jie Zhou,Bo Zhang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowRAG 提出了一种语义感知的四级异构图检索增强生成框架，通过粗粒度摘要节点、双粒度激活模块和频率感知加权流模块，解决了现有 GraphRAG 方法在抽象查询下的检索不足和多跳推理脆弱性问题，在复杂推理基准上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 方法主要基于实体级图构建，依赖隐式语义相关性传播，存在两个关键问题：(i) 当用户查询抽象且语义稀疏时，实体级别检索不足；(ii) 多跳推理脆弱，噪声激活容易干扰实体间过渡，破坏推理链，导致不可靠结论。

Method: 构建包含段落、摘要、句子和实体的四层异构图，摘要节点作为粗粒度语义枢纽。检索时采用双粒度激活模块，结合摘要-查询对齐和句子级匹配，鲁棒地激活相关实体。引入频率感知加权流模块，通过段落内词频加权实体-段落链接，剪枝噪声连接，提取高置信度推理路径作为显式逻辑骨架。

Result: 在复杂推理基准上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: FlowRAG 通过粗-细粒度语义结合的异构图设计、双粒度激活和频率感知加权流，有效提升了 GraphRAG 在抽象查询下的语义召回率和显式推理能力。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) is effective for knowledge-intensive and multi-hop query tasks; however, many existing methods primarily seed entity-based graphs and rely on implicit semantic relevance propagation. This often (i) under-retrieves when user queries are abstract and semantically sparse at the entity level, and (ii) suffers from brittle multi-hop reasoning, where noisy activations can derail entity-to-entity transitions and corrupt the inferred relation chain, yielding unreliable conclusions. To this end, we propose \texttt{FlowRAG}, a semantic-aware retrieval framework that improves both semantic recall and explicit reasoning. Specifically, \texttt{FlowRAG} constructs a quad-level heterogeneous graph over passages, summaries, sentences, and entities, where summary nodes serve as a coarse semantic hub. At retrieval time, a dual-granularity activation module combines summary--query alignment with sentence-level matching to activate relevant entities under paraphrase and abstraction robustly. We then introduce a frequency-aware weighted flow module that routes relevance through entity--passage links weighted by within-passage term frequency, pruning noisy connections and extracting high-confidence reasoning paths as an explicit logic skeleton for generation. Extensive experiments show that \texttt{FlowRAG} obtains state-of-the-art performance on complex reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [86] [StepGuard: Guarding Web Navigation via Single-Step Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17871)
*Zhihao Cui,Yuchen Zhang,Xiyang Sun,Yaxiong Wang,Li Zhu,Jinpeng Hu,Liu Liu,Mengjia Li,Yujiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了StepGuard框架，通过动态双策略优化（DDPO）解决奖励纠缠问题，以及置信度引导的自适应导航反思（CANR）校准单步误差，在网页导航任务上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有web导航方法存在单步脆弱性，主要源于奖励错配（reward misalignment）和误差传播（error propagation）导致的奖励纠缠问题。

Method: 提出StepGuard框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 动态双策略优化（DDPO），在导航优先和答案优先模式间动态切换以缓解奖励冲突；2) 置信度引导的自适应导航反思（CANR），估计每步置信度并在必要时触发反思，使用对比奖励鼓励自我纠正。

Result: 在标准web导航基准上显著提升了导航和答案准确率，取得了新的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过单步校准有效缓解了奖励纠缠和误差传播问题，验证了DDPO和CANR在web导航任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Web navigation requires agents to follow natural language goals, interact with web pages, and produce accurate answers. While recent advances leverage vision-language models and reinforcement learning, existing methods still suffer from single-step fragility due to reward misalignment and error propagation. To tackle the reward entanglement, we design Dynamic Dual-Policy Optimization (DDPO), which dynamically switches between a navigation-first mode for exploration and an answer-first mode for question-answering to mitigate reward conflict. To calibrate the single-step error, we propose Confidence-Guided Adaptive Navigation Reflection (CANR), a mechanism that estimates per-step confidence, triggers reflection only when necessary, and uses contrastive rewards to encourage self-correction to calibrate the single-step inaccuracy. With the above as the main components, we finally develop our StepGuard, a new framework of Guarding Web Navigation via Single-Step Calibration. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves navigation and answer accuracy, setting new state-of-the-art performance on standard web navigation benchmarks.

</details>


### [87] [Structural Preservation and the Logical Expressiveness of Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17882)
*Przemysław Andrzej Wałęga,Bernardo Cuenca Grau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从语义视角建立图神经网络（GNN）与逻辑形式之间的对应关系，证明在嵌入、单射同态和同态保持下的GNN分类器分别对应存在性梯度模态逻辑、其存在-正片段和存在-正模态逻辑，并通过有界高度树的良拟序结果为每种逻辑类提供了对应表达能力的GNN架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN与逻辑形式之间的桥接依赖于特定的架构选择（如聚合、组合和激活函数类型），这限制了所建立的对应关系的 generality。本文旨在从语义角度出发，不依赖具体架构选择，建立更广泛的GNN分类器类与逻辑片段之间的刻画关系。

Method: 采用语义视角，研究在嵌入、单射同态和同态三种结构性质下保持的GNN分类器类；利用有界高度树的良拟序结果，实现展开不变类的有限表示，从而建立与梯度模态逻辑各片段之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明了在嵌入、单射同态和同态保持下的GNN分类器分别由存在性梯度模态逻辑、其存在-正片段和存在-正模态逻辑刻画；同时证明了每个逻辑类都存在一个具有相同表达能力的GNN架构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了独立于具体架构选择的GNN逻辑表达力刻画，为理解GNN的语义本质提供了新的理论框架，并展示了良拟序方法在刻画GNN表达能力中的有效性。

Abstract: Bridges between graph neural networks (GNNs) and logical formalisms have been established by fixing architectural choices, such as the types of aggregation, combination, and activation functions. These choices define restricted classes of GNNs for which tight correspondences with logical formalisms can be obtained, by showing that logical formulae can be translated into equivalent GNNs and, conversely, that GNNs can be translated into equivalent formulae.
  In this paper we take a semantic perspective by establishing the logical expressiveness of classes of GNN classifiers that are preserved under structural properties: embeddings (extensions), injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms. We show that, for each such property, there exists a fragment of graded modal logic characterising the class of GNNs. In particular, preservation under embeddings, injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms corresponds to existential graded modal logic, its existential-positive fragment, and existential-positive modal logic, respectively. These results characterise the expressiveness of broad classes of GNNs independently of specific architectural choices, but we also show that each of these classes admits a GNN architecture of the same expressiveness.
  Technically, our approach uses a new well-quasi-order result for trees of bounded height, yielding finite representations of unravelling-invariant classes.

</details>


### [88] [MathVis-Fine: Aligning Visual Supervision with Necessity via Progressive Dependency-Guided Training for Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17888)
*Wanshi Xu,Haokun Zhao,Haidong Yuan,Songjun Cao,Long Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态数学推理中视觉信息依赖不足的问题，提出MathVis-Fine框架，通过细粒度视觉标注数据集和两阶段渐进式视觉增强训练范式，根据样本内在视觉依赖程度平衡答案正确性奖励与视觉定位奖励，有效提升了多模态数学推理的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT推理方法在多模态场景中将视觉输入视为同质化或辅助信号，未能捕捉文本与图像之间复杂且样本特定的依赖关系，导致视觉监督信号粗粒度且训练反馈不准确，限制了多模态推理的精确性。

Method: 1) 构建MathVis-Fine数据集，标注细粒度视觉依赖评分；2) 提出两阶段渐进式视觉增强训练范式，根据样本内在视觉依赖程度动态平衡答案正确性奖励与视觉定位奖励，以减轻奖励偏差、提升监督精度。

Result: 实验表明MathVis-Fine框架能基于视觉依赖程度逐步增强视觉感知能力，为多模态数学推理提供了更精确的训练框架。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度视觉依赖建模和渐进式训练，有效解决了多模态数学推理中视觉监督信号粗粒度、奖励偏差等问题，提升了模型在多模态数学推理中的表现。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has extended from purely linguistic domains to multimodal scenarios; however, existing approaches often treat visual inputs as homogeneous or auxiliary signals, failing to capture the intricate and sample-specific dependencies between text and images in mathematical problem-solving. This gives rise to two core issues: first, the supervisory signals for visual content are generalized and coarse-grained, lacking adaptation to the actual necessity of visual information in each sample; second, training feedback becomes inaccurate when visual rewards are uniformly applied without distinguishing the complementary relationships among inputs. These limitations hinder models from achieving precise multimodal reasoning. In this work, we propose a framework for modeling fine-grained visual dependencies in mathematical reasoning. We first construct the MathVis-Fine dataset, augmenting fine-grained visual annotations with visual dependency ratings. Building upon this dataset, we introduce a two-stage progressive visual enhancement training paradigm that balances answer correctness rewards and visual grounding rewards according to the intrinsic visual dependency level of each sample, thereby mitigating reward bias and improving supervision accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the MathVis-Fine framework effectively enhances visual perception progressively based on visual dependency, offering a more precise training framework for multimodal mathematical reasoning. We will release the dataset upon acceptance.

</details>


### [89] [Learn to Quantify Social Interaction with Constraints for Pedestrian Walking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17897)
*Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Learn to Cluster"的无标签、概率潜变量生成方法，用于量化和解释人群中的社交互动模式，并将其集成到行人轨迹预测模型中，以提升长期路径预测的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人轨迹预测方法虽考虑了社交互动，但未能揭示社交互动的具体类型及其对行人决策过程的影响，导致预测模型的鲁棒性受限。社交互动直观上数量庞大且难以标注和量化。

Method: 提出Learn to Cluster方法，通过概率潜变量生成模型，直接从序列轨迹观测中学习社交互动的聚类。该方法无需标签，可扩展至任意数量的行人，并能自然地集成到预测模型的训练过程中，潜变量作为"标签"对社交互动进行分类。

Result: 在多个轨迹预测基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法能够有效学习社交互动模式，并将这些模式成功集成到行人轨迹预测中。

Conclusion: 通过无监督方式量化社交互动并融入预测模型，能够有效提升轨迹预测的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-term human path forecasting in crowds is critical for autonomous moving platforms (like autonomous driving cars and social robots) to avoid collision and make high-quality planning. Although the current research take into account social interactions for prediction, they don't reveal the exact kinds of social interactions happened among people and how the social interactions affect the decision-making process of pedestrians, which further limits its robustness. Social interactions in pedestrian walking are intuitively massive and hard to label and quantify. In this paper, we explore creatively to quantify and interpret how pedestrians interact with others by proposing Learn to Cluster. Our clustering social interactions is probabilistic latent variable generative, learning directly from sequential trajectory observations, scalable to arbitrary number of pedestrians. Learn to cluster is label-free and can be naturally integrated into the training process of the prediction model. The latent variables will then serve as 'labels' to categorize social interactions. Extensive experiments over several trajectory prediction benchmarks demonstrate that our method is able to learn the patterns of social interactions and effectively integrate the patterns to pedestrian trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [90] [DiagFlowBench: Evaluating How Language Models Handle Off-Procedure Inputs in Grounded Diagnostic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17904)
*Guillermo Gil de Avalle,Laura Maruster,Shaina Raza,Christos Emmanouilidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiagFlowBench，一个基于工业诊断流程图的 50 组流程图、1676 轮多轮对话数据集，用于评估语言模型在处理超出范围查询时的正确拒答能力。实验发现模型普遍倾向于选择真实但上下文不当的步骤，而非直接编造信息，暴露了接地系统在面对“映射但错误”建议时的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少关注对话中模型需识别超出范围输入的动态场景，而实际运维中操作员查询常偏离标准流程，因此需要专门的基准来评估模型的拒答能力。

Method: 从消费制造商收集 50 张工业诊断流程图，构建 1676 轮多轮对话，对比合规与超出范围的用户语句。使用 10 个商业和开源模型进行评测。

Result: 模型在拒答率上存在高度差异；模型很少编造事实，但倾向于选择一个真实但在上下文中不恰当的操作步骤，这种“映射错误”建议具有内在的可信性和权威性，构成严峻的安全隐患。

Conclusion: 当前接地系统对超出范围查询的防护存在显著漏洞，模型选择真实但错误的步骤比直接幻觉更隐蔽、更具危害性，需要针对此脆弱性设计更有效的防护机制。

Abstract: Language models increasingly serve as advisory systems in maintenance operations. To prevent hallucination, recent systems ground these models in procedural documentation to constrain them to approved steps. In practice, however, operator queries frequently stray from this path, requiring models to recognise out-of-scope inputs mid-conversation, a dynamic that current benchmarks rarely prioritise. We introduce DiagFlowBench, a dataset of 50 industrial diagnostic flowcharts from a consumer manufacturer converted into 1,676 multi-turn conversations that contrast compliant with out-of-scope utterances. Evaluating a panel of ten commercial and open-weight models reveals high variability in abstention rates, with models commonly selecting a real but contextually inadequate step rather than fabricating facts. The inherent plausibility and authority of this mapped but wrong advice exposes a challenging vulnerability for grounding systems.

</details>


### [91] [PreAct: Computer-Using Agents that Get Faster on Repeated Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17929)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PreAct通过将成功的任务执行编译为状态机程序，使计算机使用代理在重复任务上实现8.5-13倍加速，无需每次调用语言模型，并在执行过程中持续验证屏幕状态以保证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理每次执行任务都从零开始（重新读取屏幕、重新推理操作），即使是重复任务也需付出完整计算成本，效率低下。

Method: 首次成功执行任务时，将运行轨迹编译为小型状态机程序（状态检查屏幕、转换执行动作）；后续重复执行时直接回放该程序，无需每步调用语言模型。回放过程中每步验证屏幕状态与预期匹配，否则交回控制权。新程序需通过独立评估器从干净状态重新运行确认任务完成才入库。

Result: 实现8.5-13倍加速，无每步语言模型调用；存储时验证可分离有效/无效程序，带来1.75-2.6个任务的提升；在移动、桌面和网页三个基准上表现一致；无程序匹配时回退到探索策略使其与强记录-回放基线持平。

Conclusion: PreAct有效提升重复任务执行效率，存储时验证是关键组件，而提示词措辞、运行时护栏、程序检索方式（语言模型或嵌入检索）对结果影响不大。

Abstract: Computer-using agents drive real software through the screen -- clicking and typing -- but they solve every task from scratch: asked to repeat a task, an agent re-reads the screen, re-reasons every tap, and pays the full cost again. We present PreAct, which lets such an agent get faster on tasks it has done before. The first time it succeeds, PreAct compiles the run into a small state-machine program-states that check the screen, transitions that act-and on later runs replays it directly instead of invoking the agent 8.5-13x faster, with no per-step language-model calls. Replay is not blind: at each step PreAct checks that the screen matches what the program expects before acting, and hands control back to the agent the moment something is off.
  PreAct applies the same discipline when deciding what to keep: a freshly compiled program enters the store only if, re-run from a clean state, an independent evaluator confirms it solved the task-catching programs that replay to their last step yet leave the task undone. Across a mobile, a desktop, and a web benchmark, this store-time check separates repeated runs that improve from ones that degrade as faulty programs accumulate, worth 1.75-2.6 tasks per benchmark, the same direction on all three; a fallback that explores afresh when no program fits brings PreAct level with a strong record-and-replay baseline. We also report what did not matter: prompt wording, runtime guardrails, and whether a language model or a plain embedding retriever selects which program to reuse.

</details>


### [92] [How Inference Compute Shapes Frontier LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17930)
*Jessica McFadyen,Ole Jorgensen,Harry Coppock,Kevin Wei,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理时计算资源（token预算、上下文压缩、重复提交尝试）对12个前沿语言模型在7个挑战性基准上的性能影响，发现更大的推理预算能显著提升模型表现，固定预算评估会低估模型真实能力，且不同基准对推理扩展方法的敏感度各异。作者主张评估应报告作为推理时计算函数的性能，并明确协议选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估趋向于需要长轨迹和工具使用的复杂任务，模型性能对推理时计算量越来越敏感。然而，许多评估仍然在单一且受限的计算预算下报告性能，导致低分可能反映的是评估设置问题而非模型真实能力。作者旨在检验这一假设，并量化推理计算对模型评估的影响。

Method: 在受控设置下评估12个前沿语言模型在7个挑战性基准（涵盖软件工程、数学、医学和网络安全）上的表现。使用三种简单的推理扩展干预措施：更大的token预算、上下文压缩、以及基于模型自身或最小正确性反馈引导的重复提交尝试。

Result: 三个主要发现：1）更大的token预算在网络安全、FrontierMath、Humanity's Last Exam和TerminalBench等多个领域显著提升性能；2）固定预算评估会越来越低估前沿模型的能力，新模型在大预算下能解锁更难任务并更可靠地解决；3）不同基准对推理扩展方法的偏好不同——重复提交普遍有效，但更大token预算、外部反馈和并行尝试的价值因基准而异。

Conclusion: 基准分数是协议依赖的。评估应报告作为推理时计算函数的性能，明确指定协议选择，并在匹配预算的大共享计算范围内比较模型代际，特别是在安全或政策相关的场景中。

Abstract: AI evaluations are shifting toward harder tasks that benefit from longer trajectories involving tool use and iterative problem solving. As a result, performance is increasingly sensitive to the amount and allocation of compute available at test time ("inference compute"). Yet many evaluations still report performance at a single restrictive budget, meaning that low scores may reflect the evaluation setup rather than the model's underlying capability. To test this, we evaluate up to 12 frontier language models on seven challenging benchmarks spanning software engineering, mathematics, medicine, and cybersecurity. We use a controlled setup combining three simple inference-scaling interventions: larger token budgets, context compaction, and repeated submission attempts, guided either by the model itself or by minimal correctness feedback. We find three main results. First, larger token budgets substantially improve performance on benchmarks across multiple domains, including cybersecurity, FrontierMath, Humanity's Last Exam, and TerminalBench. Second, fixed-budget evaluations can increasingly understate frontier capability as models advance. Newer models reach higher performance at large budgets, where they unlock harder tasks and solve them more reliably. Third, benchmarks differ in which inference-scaling methods help most: repeated submission broadly improves performance, but the value of larger token budgets, external feedback, and parallel attempts varies by benchmark. Overall, our results show that benchmark scores are protocol-dependent. We therefore argue that evaluations should report capability as a function of inference-time compute, specify protocol choices explicitly, and compare model generations over a large shared compute range at matched budgets, especially in safety- or policy-relevant settings.

</details>


### [93] [Small Initialization Matters for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17945)
*Liangkai Hang,Junjie Yao,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Hongkang Yang,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现参数初始化尺度是影响大语言模型预训练和能力的关键因素，小初始化能显著提升预训练效果尤其是推理任务，并提出了γ-初始化规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型中智能涌现的机制，超越传统的规模、数据和架构视角，研究参数初始化如何像基因一样决定模型训练和能力。

Method: 通过控制初始化尺度进行对比实验，识别抑制小初始化优势的常见经验设置，分析不同初始化下的模型发育轨迹和token级预测行为。

Result: 小初始化持续改善预训练效果，在推理密集型任务上增益最大；存在一个平衡推理与训练的临界初始化值；小初始化驱动"先压缩后扩展"的发育轨迹，且增益集中在非平凡、上下文约束的预测上。

Conclusion: 提出γ-初始化规则：将初始化范围作为显式超参数，默认使用小初始化——一种几乎零成本的干预措施，可在不同模型规模下改善预训练并增强推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models provide a tractable system for asking how intelligence itself emerges, rather than only how LLMs can be engineered. Although progress is usually attributed to scale, data and architecture, we show that parameter initialization is a gene-like determinant of training and, in particular, of model capacity. Reducing the initialization scale consistently improves pretraining, with the largest gains on reasoning-demanding tasks. We identify two widely used empirical settings that restrain the advantage of small initialization, and show how relaxing them restores favorable scaling. We further uncover a critical initialization that balances the reasoning and training. Mechanistically, small initialization drives a distinct developmental trajectory: parameters first condense into low-complexity structures and later expand into richer representations, giving concrete form to the idea that compression is intelligence. Token-level analyses show that the gains concentrate on non-trivial, context-constrained predictions rather than all tokens uniformly. These results motivate a simple $γ$-initialization rule: expose initialization rage as an explicit knob and use small initialization by default, an almost cost-free intervention that improves pretraining and strengthens reasoning across model scales.

</details>


### [94] [MoCo-AIS: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Similarity Computation of Vessel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17978)
*Ruixin Song,Md Mahbub Alam,Zahra Sadeghi,Amilcar Soares,José F. Rodrigues-Jr,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoCo-AIS，一个基于动量对比(MoCo)范式的统一框架，用于学习船舶轨迹嵌入表示。该框架通过正负轨迹对进行相似性学习，并在大规模真实AIS数据集上评估了多种主流深度学习模型，实验证明该框架显著优于现有基准，同时为轨迹表示模型提供了基准测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于距离的轨迹相似度计算方法计算成本高，监督方法依赖大量标签且泛化性差，自监督对比学习缺乏统一框架，难以对深度学习模型进行一致的轨迹表示比较。

Method: 基于动量对比(MoCo)范式构建统一框架，通过构建正负轨迹对进行自监督对比学习，并在大规模真实船舶跟踪AIS数据集上评估多种主流深度学习模型。

Result: 该框架在轨迹相似性学习上显著优于现有基线方法，并为评估轨迹表示模型提供了可靠的基准测试平台。

Conclusion: MoCo-AIS通过统一的对比学习框架有效解决了轨迹相似性学习的泛化问题和模型比较难题，在真实大规模AIS数据上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Trajectory similarity is a fundamental task in analyzing mobility patterns, essential for applications such as route pattern extraction, mobility prediction, and anomaly detection. Traditional distance-based measures for computing similarity incur high computational cost, driving the adoption of lightweight learning-based approaches. Supervised methods rely on extensive labels derived from traditional distance measures and often reproduce these metrics, which limits generalization. While self-supervised learning addresses this issue through contrastive learning, it lacks a unified framework, making it difficult to compare deep learning (DL) models for consistent trajectory representation. Accordingly, this paper presents MoCo-AIS, a unified framework for learning vessel trajectory embeddings based on the Momentum Contrast (MoCo) paradigm, which formulates similarity learning through positive and negative trajectory pairs. Within this framework, we evaluate a diverse set of leading DL models on large-scale, real-world vessel-tracking AIS datasets that capture diverse navigation behaviors and operating conditions. Results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves similarity learning over existing baselines, while providing a benchmarking platform for evaluating trajectory representation models.

</details>


### [95] [STAR: SpatioTemporal Adaptive Reward Allocation for Text-to-Image RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17979)
*Jinjie Shen,Wei Deng,Xian Hu,Daiguo Zhou,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有文本到图像生成的RL后训练方法通常将最终图像奖励转化为单一标量优势值，并均匀应用于整个生成轨迹。针对这一粒度不匹配问题，本文提出STAR（时空自适应奖励分配）方法，利用生成模型内的文本-图像注意力，构建动态空间分配图，将奖励更准确地分配给相关潜在区域，并在GenEval、OCR文本渲染和PickScore三项任务上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法将最终图像奖励转化为单一标量优势值，以相同强度应用于整个生成轨迹，忽略了文本到图像生成固有的时空结构——不同去噪步骤负责不同生成阶段，且真正决定文本对齐的内容通常只出现在部分图像区域。这种粒度不匹配使得策略更新难以聚焦于实际影响奖励的生成组件。

Method: STAR方法利用生成模型内的文本-图像注意力，从用户提示中的核心内容出发，构建随去噪步骤和轨迹动态变化的时空分配图，将相同的分组相对优势奖励分配给更相关的潜在区域。随后通过空间解析的策略目标对这些区域施加更强的策略更新，几乎无需额外计算开销。

Result: 以Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium为基座模型，在GenEval、OCR文本渲染和PickScore三项任务上分别达到0.9759、0.9757和23.60，在组合语义对齐、文本渲染和偏好优化方面均显著提升。

Conclusion: STAR通过细粒度的时空自适应奖励分配有效解决了RL后训练中奖励与生成结构不匹配的问题，在不改变外部奖励源的情况下提升了文本到图像生成的各方面表现，且计算开销极低。

Abstract: Existing RL post-training methods for text-to-image generation usually convert the final-image reward into a single scalar advantage and apply it with the same strength to the entire generative trajectory. However, text-to-image generation naturally has temporal and spatial structure: different denoising steps are responsible for different generation stages, and the content that truly determines text alignment often appears only in part of the image. This granularity mismatch makes it difficult for policy updates to focus on the generative components that actually affect the reward. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{SpatioTemporal Adaptive Reward (STAR) Allocation} for RL post-training of text-to-image diffusion and flow models. STAR uses text-image attention inside the generative model and starts from the core content that the user truly cares about in the prompt. It constructs spatial allocation maps that dynamically vary across denoising steps and rollouts, and allocates the same group-relative advantage to more relevant latent regions with almost no additional computational overhead. STAR then applies stronger policy updates to these regions through a spatially resolved policy objective. We use Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium as the base model and evaluate on three tasks: GenEval, OCR text rendering, and PickScore. Experimental results show that STAR improves compositional semantic alignment, text rendering, and preference optimization without changing the external reward source, achieving $\mathbf{0.9759}$, $\mathbf{0.9757}$, and $\mathbf{23.60}$ on GenEval, OCR, and PickScore, respectively.

</details>


### [96] [LLM Consumer Behavior Theory: Foundations of a Novel Research Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18005)
*Manon Reusens,Sofie Goethals,David Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"LLM消费者行为理论"这一新领域，将经济学与NLP结合，系统分析LLM作为自主代理在市场中代表用户进行消费决策的行为模式、偏好表达及市场聚合机制，并指出代理市场中对理性假设、异质性等传统经济学假设的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被部署为替用户做消费决策的自主代理，传统消费者理论（以人类为决策主体）面临根本性挑战，亟需建立新的理论框架来理解代理化市场中的消费者行为。

Method: 融合经典与行为经济学理论及NLP最新进展，统一梳理LLM决策、人类行为模拟和偏好引导等碎片化文献，从经济学视角系统构建理论框架。

Result: 提出了LLM消费者行为理论的学科范畴，明确了代理市场中理性假设和异质性假设可能失效的关键问题，识别了偏好对齐、偏好表示和市场动态等开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文为代理化市场中的消费者行为研究提供了理论纲领，指出未来需在偏好对齐、偏好表示和市场动力学三个核心方向开展进一步探索。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that make consumption decisions on behalf of users. This shift raises fundamental questions for consumer theory, which has traditionally modeled humans as the primary decision-makers. In this paper, we introduce LLM Consumer Behavior Theory, a new field of study concerned with analyzing consumer behavior in agentic markets. Drawing on classical and behavioral economics alongside recent advances in Natural Language Processing, we formalize how human preferences are reflected and acted upon by LLM-based agents, and how agent-level decisions aggregate into market demand. We unify previously fragmented literature on LLM decision-making, human behavior simulation, and preference elicitation under a common economic lens, highlighting where assumptions, such as rationality and heterogeneity, may fail in agentic markets. Rather than providing empirical validation, this paper outlines the scope of LLM consumer behavior and identifies open research questions related to alignment, preference representation, and market dynamics.

</details>


### [97] [LegalHalluLens: Typed Hallucination Auditing and Calibrated Multi-Agent Debate for Trustworthy Legal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18021)
*Lalit Yadav,Akshaj Gurugubelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LegalHalluLens是一个针对法律AI幻觉的审计框架，通过类型化幻觉画像、风险方向指数(RDI)和类型化辩论管道三大组件，揭示了聚合指标(如52%幻觉率)掩盖的关键差异：义务/数字类与时间类声明间存在约38-40个百分点的幻觉率差距，且两个报告相同52%幻觉率的系统可能具有相反的RDI值。辩论管道将虚假检测减少45%，使用仅4B参数的轻量级模型即可匹配商业API性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法律AI系统幻觉率聚合指标(~52%)掩盖了错误在哪些类别中集中以及偏向哪个方向的关键信息，导致合规官无法获得可操作的信号来判断系统是否值得信赖部署。

Method: 提出LegalHalluLens审计框架，包含：(1)基于CUAD数据集构建四种法律类别(数值、时间、义务/权益、事实)的类型化幻觉画像；(2)风险方向指数(RDI)将遗漏偏差与发明偏差压缩为可比较的标量；(3)类型化辩论管道，校准幅度和方向，采用怀疑者挑战和非对称门控机制。

Result: 在510份合同、249,252个条款级实例上，发现相同模型内义务/数字类与时间类声明间幻觉率差距约38-40个百分点；两个报告相同52%幻觉率的系统可能具有相反的RDI；辩论管道将虚假检测减少45%，不同类别的增益与诊断一致，使用4B参数的轻量级模型即可匹配商业API。

Conclusion: 类型化画像和RDI能揭示聚合指标隐藏的故障模式，这些诊断可作为多智能体辩论管道的校准输入，支持法律AI在现实部署中的方向感知采购、问责和智能体设计。

Abstract: AI systems deployed in legal workflows hallucinate at rates that aggregate metrics report at ~52%, but this average conceals where errors concentrate and in which direction they run, leaving compliance officers without an actionable signal for trustworthy deployment. We present LegalHalluLens, an auditing framework with three components: typed hallucination profiles across four legally-motivated claim categories (numeric, temporal, obligation/entitlement, factual) over CUAD (Hendrycks et al., 2021); a Risk Direction Index (RDI) that reduces omission-versus-invention bias to a single deployment-comparable scalar; and a typed debate pipeline calibrated to both magnitudes and directions. Across 510 contracts and 249,252 clause-level instances we measure a within-model gap of approximately 38-40 pp between obligation/numeric and temporal claims that aggregate reporting hides, and show that two systems with matched 52% rates can carry opposite RDIs. The debate pipeline reduces fabricated detections by 45% with per-category gains tracking the diagnosis, matching commercial APIs with a substantially smaller backbone (4B active parameters). Typed profiles and RDI surface failure modes that aggregate metrics hide; we further show these diagnostics serve as calibration inputs for multi-agent debate pipelines, where Skeptic challenges and asymmetric gates targeted at measured failure modes outperform generically-tuned debate. The framework supports direction-aware procurement, accountability, and agent design for legal AI deployed in the wild.

</details>


### [98] [ProvenanceGuard: Source-Aware Factuality Verification for MCP-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18037)
*Ander Alvarez,Santhiya Rajan,Samuel Mugel,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProvenanceGuard，一种面向MCP协议工具调用型LLM智能体的溯源感知验证器，用于检测跨源混淆（将正确信息归因到错误来源）这一事实性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准事实性指标仅检验答案是否被汇总证据支持，忽略了溯源敏感的失败模式：一条声明可能在某个源中得到支持，却被归因到了错误的来源（跨源混淆）。

Method: ProvenanceGuard：捕获MCP轨迹（含工具ID、源ID和原始输出）→将答案分解为原子声明→路由到对应源证据→使用NLI和令牌对齐代理验证支持度→比较声明的归因与路由后的源→返回逐声明判定及答案级允许/阻止决策。阻止的答案可通过检索增强的答案修订和重新验证进行修复。

Result: 在281个医学MCP轨迹上评估，40个保留轨迹上阻止F1=0.802、源准确率=0.858；多源困难基准上阻止F1=0.846，源+关系准确率降至0.229；修复流程可解决所有被阻止答案；50个受控临床混淆探测中检测出全部注入的归因交换。

Conclusion: 源归因是基于MCP的智能体事实性验证的一个独立维度，现有源盲方法无法检测跨源混淆问题。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly use the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to answer from heterogeneous evidence sources, including search, APIs, databases, clinical records, and formulary tools. Standard factuality metrics usually test whether an answer is supported by pooled evidence, missing a provenance-sensitive failure mode: a claim may be supported somewhere while being attributed to the wrong source. We call this cross-source conflation.
  We introduce ProvenanceGuard, a source-aware verifier for MCP-grounded answers. It consumes captured MCP traces with stable tool IDs, source IDs, and raw outputs; decomposes answers into atomic claims; routes claims to source-specific evidence; checks support with NLI and a token-alignment proxy; compares stated attribution with the routed source; and returns per-claim verdicts plus an answer-level allow/block decision. Blocked answers can be repaired with retrieval-augmented answer revision and re-verified.
  We evaluate on 281 medical-domain MCP-agent traces. A 266-trace adjudicated subset yields 2,325 LLM-assisted claim labels split by trace; 361 held-out labels are human-verified. On the 40-trace held-out split, ProvenanceGuard achieves block F1 0.802 and source accuracy 0.858 over 260 source-eligible claims, outperforming source-blind baselines that do not emit claim-to-source IDs. On a harder multi-source benchmark it reaches block F1 0.846, while source-plus-relation accuracy drops to 0.229, showing that exact source ownership remains difficult with semantically close sources. Repair-and-reverify resolves all blocked answers in the full trace set, often via conservative fallback. In 50 controlled clinical conflation probes, ProvenanceGuard detects all injected attribution swaps with no retained wrong attribution. These results show that source attribution is an independent axis for factuality verification in MCP-based agents.

</details>


### [99] [PseudoBench: Measuring How Agentic Auto-Research Fuels Pseudoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18060)
*Xinyang Liao,Lingyu Li,Huacan Liu,Tianle Gu,Yang Yao,Tong Zhu,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PseudoBench基准，用于评估AI科研代理抵抗伪科学的能力。测试7个前沿代理发现，它们极易生成支持伪科学的有说服力报告，拒绝率近乎为零，最高抵抗率仅27.4%，且更强的代理会用更专业的科学语言包装伪科学，增加其表面可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的代理进入自主科学研究领域，它们抵抗伪科学的能力变得至关重要，否则可能快速生成看似合理但具有误导性的研究，污染学术文献并侵蚀科学信任。

Method: 构建PseudoBench基准，包含200个跨5个领域的伪科学声称-证据对，通过端到端研究流程（从实验到写作）评估代理。测试7个最先进的代理系统。

Result: 当前代理系统极易生成与伪科学前提一致的有说服力报告，拒绝率近乎为零，最高抵抗率仅27.4%。更强的代理反而用更复杂的科学语言包装伪科学，增加其表面可信度。

Conclusion: 现有AI科研代理存在惊人的助长伪科学的能力，在广泛部署前需要进行科学对齐。

Abstract: As Large Language Model based agents enter autonomous scientific research, their ability to resist pseudoscience becomes increasingly important. Otherwise, such systems may rapidly generate plausible yet misleading studies that contaminate academic literature and erode trust in science. We present PseudoBench, an adversarial benchmark for evaluating whether agentic auto-research systems can identify and resist pseudoscientific narratives. PseudoBench contains 200 curated pseudoscientific claim-evidence pairs across five domains and evaluates agents through an end-to-end research pipeline from experiments to writing. Testing seven state-of-the-art agents, we find that current systems readily produce persuasive reports that align with pseudoscientific premises with near-zero refusal rates and the highest resistance of only 27.4%. Stronger agents risk packaging pseudoscience in more sophisticated scientific language, increasing its apparent credibility. These findings reveal an alarming capacity to fuel pseudoscience, calling for scientific alignment before widespread deployment.

</details>


### [100] [Agentic AI-based Framework for Mitigating Premature Diagnostic Handoff and Silent Hallucination in Healthcare Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18068)
*Divyansh Srivastava,Shreya Ghosh,Anshul Verma,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多智能体框架，通过确定性编排约束替代"LLM即裁判"路由，并引入神经符号状态追踪门（确保OLDCARTS临床协议完整性）和认知不确定性量化门（基于语义熵拦截分歧输出）两种安全机制，在150个测试用例上将诊断精确度从38.0%提升至49.3%，并发现OLDCARTS完整性与语义熵之间存在显著负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的开放域对话智能体在医疗推理中存在两个关键故障模式：过早诊断移交（premature diagnostic handoff）和可能在到达患者前未被检测到的沉默临床幻觉（silent clinical hallucinations），亟需通过系统性约束机制加以解决。

Method: 提出多智能体框架，核心包含两个安全门控机制：(1) 神经符号状态追踪门，强制要求OLDCARTS协议（发病、位置、持续时间、特征、加重/缓解因素、放射、时机、严重程度）所有维度收集完整后方可进行诊断转换；(2) 认知不确定性量化门，对K=5个独立诊断样本计算语义熵（H）以识别并拦截分歧输出。采用llama-3.1-70b-instruct模型在150个测试案例上进行评估。

Result: 完整架构达到49.3%的诊断精确度，相比无约束基线（38.0%）绝对提升11.3个百分点。OLDCARTS完整性（σ）与语义熵（H）之间存在统计显著的负相关（r=-0.181, p<0.05），表明结构化信息收集与诊断不确定性降低相关联。

Conclusion: 确定性编排约束与双门控安全机制能有效提升LLM多智能体系统的诊断可靠性，结构化临床协议信息收集有助于降低诊断不确定性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and multi-agent systems have driven the rise of Agentic AI, showing promise for medical reasoning. However, open-ended conversational agents remain prone to two critical failure modes: premature diagnostic handoff and silent clinical hallucinations that may go undetected before reaching the patient. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that addresses both issues by replacing ``LLM-as-a-judge'' routing with deterministic orchestration constraints. The framework incorporates two safety mechanisms. First, a neuro-symbolic state-tracking gate enforces completeness of the OLDCARTS clinical protocol (Onset, Location, Duration, Character, Aggravating/Alleviating factors, Radiation, Timing, and Severity) by blocking diagnostic transitions until all required dimensions are collected. Second, an epistemic uncertainty quantification (UQ) gate computes semantic entropy (H) across K=5 independent diagnostic samples to identify and intercept divergent outputs before delivery.
  We evaluate the system using simulated patient agents powered by the llama-3.1-70b-instruct model on 150 test cases. The full architecture achieves 49.3% diagnostic precision, representing an absolute improvement of 11.3 percentage points over an unconstrained baseline. Additionally, we observe a statistically significant negative correlation (r = -0.181, p < 0.05) between OLDCARTS completeness (σ) and semantic entropy (H), suggesting that structured information gathering is associated with reduced diagnostic uncertainty.

</details>


### [101] [A Unified Framework for Context-Aware and Relation-Aware Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18075)
*Haoyang Zhong,Yifei Sun,Antong Zhang,Chunping Wang,Lei Chen,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyGRAG提出了一种层次化图RAG框架，通过在混合图上构建层次索引、迭代聚类生成LLM摘要、设计上下文和关系感知的检索机制以及支持动态知识更新，在多跳推理任务上平均准确率提升9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的RAG方法存在根本性局限：无论是实体为中心还是片段为中心的方法，其表示都锚定于原始文本，缺乏真正的知识融合。两者都通过相似性搜索独立检索信息，无法从信息综合中产生涌现性理解。

Method: (1) 在包含片段和实体节点的混合图上设计层次化索引结构；(2) 迭代聚类并生成基于LLM的摘要；(3) 设计上下文和关系感知的检索机制，跨所有抽象层级搜索并通过社区成员关系扩展；(4) 通过基于附件的算法实现动态知识更新，仅需局部重新摘要。

Result: HyGRAG在多跳推理任务上平均准确率提升9.7%，同时保持了合理的效率。

Conclusion: HyGRAG通过层次化图结构和综合摘要生成有效解决了现有RAG方法缺乏知识融合的问题，在提高多跳推理准确性的同时支持动态更新。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a paradigm for enhancing large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge, yet existing graph-based methods face a fundamental limitation: entity-centric and chunk-centric approaches operate on representations anchored to original text without true knowledge fusion. While entity-centric methods connect logically related content and chunk-centric methods preserve context, both retrieve information separately through similarity search, missing emergent understanding from their synthesis. In this paper, we propose HyGRAG, a hierarchical graph RAG framework that transcends source documents by addressing three core challenges: constructing summaries that genuinely integrate contextual and relational information, leveraging these synthesized representations to access emergent knowledge during retrieval, and efficiently updating hierarchical structures for dynamic corpora. Specifically, we design hierarchical index structures over hybrid graphs with both chunk and entity nodes, then iteratively cluster them and generate LLM-based summaries. Then, we design context and relation-aware retrieval that searches across all abstraction levels while expanding through community membership. Moreover, we enable dynamic knowledge update through attachment-based algorithms with only local re-summarization. Experimental results show that HyGRAG improves the average accuracy of multi-hop reasoning tasks by 9.7%, while maintaining reasonable efficiency.

</details>


### [102] [IsabeLLM: Automated Theorem Proving Applied to Formally Verifying Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18098)
*Elliot Jones,William Knottenbelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文改进IsabeLLM工具，通过RAG框架、错误追踪、反例生成和兼容新版Isabelle/Sledgehammer，提升了比特币工作量证明共识的形式化验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链系统频繁遭受攻击导致巨大经济损失，亟需更好的形式化验证手段；AI可自动化定理证明工作，降低形式化验证门槛，使非安全关键系统也能受益。

Method: 对IsabeLLM进行三项改进：1) 实现检索增强生成(RAG)框架；2) 错误追踪与反例生成以优化LLM的上下文信息；3) 兼容最新版Isabelle和Sledgehammer以提高效率。

Result: 对比新旧版本IsabeLLM在完成比特币工作量证明共识验证任务中的性能表现。

Conclusion: 改进后的IsabeLLM在区块链共识协议的形式化验证任务上表现更优，验证了RAG、错误追踪等技术对AI定理证明工具的增强效果。

Abstract: Advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have led AI for Theorem Proving to become a promising means of formally verifying computer systems. Whilst formal verification is traditionally reserved for safety-critical systems due to the required amount of expertise and effort, AI can help to automate a large amount of this workload and make it far more accessible. Blockchain-based systems are becoming increasingly popular and are frequently targeted by malicious actors, often resulting in huge financial losses, highlighting the need to better verify these systems and mitigate vulnerabilities. Arguably the most important component of these systems is the consensus protocol, which allows nodes to agree on decisions in a potentially adversarial environment. In this paper, we improve upon IsabeLLM, the automated theorem proving tool in Isabelle. Namely, we implement a Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework, Error tracing and counterexample generation for improved context supplied to the Large Language Model. Compatibility with the latest version of Isabelle and Sledgehammer is also implemented for improved efficiency. We compare the performance of the two versions of IsabeLLM in their ability to complete the verification of Bitcoin's Proof of Work consensus.

</details>


### [103] [Trust the Right Teacher: Quality-Aware Self-Distillation for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18101)
*Jingyuan Huang,Zuming Huang,Yucheng Shi,Tianze Yang,Xiaoming Zhai,Wei Chu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种质量感知自蒸馏方法，通过软正确性感知门控和教师概率缩放来改进GUI接地任务中坐标令牌的教师信号质量，在六个基准测试中一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的在线策略自蒸馏(OPSD)在GUI接地任务中效果不佳，因为当学生生成的前缀偏离目标坐标时，坐标令牌的教师信号质量会下降，导致不可靠的监督信号。

Method: 提出质量感知自蒸馏，包含两个互补组件：1) 软正确性感知门控，检查在当前学生前缀下教师的坐标令牌预测是否能被完成为真实框，若不能则降低对应教师信号的权重；2) 教师概率缩放，利用教师置信度来进一步校准门控监督的强度。

Result: 在六个GUI接地基准测试上，该方法一致提升了基础模型性能，并优于强基线方法。关键发现是单独使用任一组件都无法提升整体性能，只有两者结合才能持续改进。

Conclusion: 正确性感知门控抑制不可靠的坐标令牌监督，教师概率缩放校准剩余信号的强度，两者互补共同提升GUI接地性能。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to identify small target elements in high-resolution screenshots and predict precise screen coordinates. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising post-training approach for this coordinate-sensitive task, since it provides dense token-level teacher signals beyond hard coordinate labels. However, naive OPSD is not well suited to GUI grounding: OPSD evaluates the teacher on student-generated prefixes, the quality of coordinate-token teacher signals can degrade when the prefix has already deviated from the target coordinate, leading to unreliable teacher signal. To mitigate this, We propose quality-aware self-distillation for VLM-based GUI grounding, which improves coordinate-token teacher-signal quality through soft correctness-aware gating and teacher-probability scaling. The soft correctness-aware gate checks whether the teacher's current coordinate-token prediction can still be completed into the ground-truth box under the student-generated prefix. If not, the corresponding teacher signal is down-weighted. Teacher-probability scaling then uses the teacher's confidence as a lightweight factor to further calibrate the strength of the gated supervision. A key empirical finding is that neither component alone improves overall performance, whereas combining them consistently improves performance. This suggests that the two mechanisms play complementary roles: correctness-aware gating suppresses unreliable coordinate-token supervision, while teacher-probability scaling calibrates the strength of the remaining signals. Experiments across six GUI grounding benchmarks show that our method consistently improves the base model and outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [104] [First Proof Second Batch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18119)
*Mohammed Abouzaid,Nikhil Srivastava,Rachel Ward,Lauren Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了多个当前AI系统在十个研究级数学问题上的解题能力，这些问题来自不同数学领域且源自研究过程。文章包含问题、方法、测试结果，并附有人类解答、AI生成解答及评审报告的链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估当前AI系统能否正确解决研究级别的数学问题，检验其在真实研究场景中的数学推理能力。

Method: 选取十位数学家各自在研究过程中自然产生的问题，覆盖多个数学领域；将这些问题提交给多个AI系统求解；收集AI生成的解答并由评审专家进行评估。

Result: 文章提供了测试结果，并附有AI生成解答、人类解答及评审报告的详细补充材料。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在研究级数学问题上的表现还有待提升，该测试为评估AI数学推理能力提供了系统性的基准和公开数据集。

Abstract: To assess the ability of current AI systems to correctly solve research-level mathematics problems, we tested several AI systems on a set of ten problems in a broad range of mathematical fields; these problems arose naturally in the research process of the contributors. This document includes the problems, our methodology, and the results of our testing. We provide links to supplementary documents including the human solutions, the AI-generated solutions, and the referee reports and logs for the AI-generated solutions.
  The ten problems were contributed by the following mathematicians: (1) Dariusz Kalociński and Theodore A. Slaman, (2) Richard Schwartz, (3) Aleksa Milojevic and Benny Sudakov, (4) Larry Guth, (5) Oleg Butkovsky, Jonathan Mattingly, and Lorenzo Zambotti, (6) Joshua Evan Greene and Duncan McCoy, (7) Sucharit Sarkar, (8) Sam Payne and Jidong (Jayden) Wang, (9) Sylvie Corteel and John Lentfer, (10) Srivatsav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli.

</details>


### [105] [Knowledge Reutilization in Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18132)
*Yuan Meng,Bo Wang,Juan de los Rios Ruiz,Xiangtong Yao,Zhenshan Bing,Fuchun Sun,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种元知识重用框架，在动力学简化智能体上学习任务级知识，通过语义-幅度接口和时间适配器将冻结的元知识迁移到异构智能体，实现了跨实体快速适应，最终步长追踪误差降低94.75%-99.79%，仅需约23.8%的交互数据即可达到可比部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端元强化学习方法将任务推理与具身控制耦合，导致非参数任务语义模糊、样本效率低、跨智能体重用受限。

Method: 1) 在动力学简化智能体上学习元知识；2) 使用贝叶斯非参数先验组织隐式任务模式；3) 高层策略生成任务级幅度引导；4) 语义-幅度接口和时间适配器将冻结元知识转换为时间对齐的子目标，供具身特定底层控制器使用。

Result: 在多个运动智能体上，最终步长追踪误差相比SOTA降低94.75%-99.79%，仅需约23.8%交互数据即可达到可比部署性能。

Conclusion: 解耦任务知识与具身控制、跨异构智能体重用元知识是可行且高效的，显著提升了样本效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning enables fast adaptation by extracting shared structure from related tasks, but existing end-to-end methods often couple task inference with embodiment-specific control. This coupling can obscure non-parametric task semantics, reduce sample efficiency, and limit cross-agent reuse. We propose a meta-knowledge reutilization framework that learns task-level knowledge on a dynamics-simplified agent and transfers it to heterogeneous agents. The framework uses a Bayesian non-parametric prior to organize latent task modes and a high-level policy to generate task-level magnitude guidance. To bridge reusable task knowledge with different embodiments, we introduce a semantic-magnitude interface and a lightweight temporal adaptor, which convert frozen meta-knowledge into temporally aligned subgoals for embodiment-specific low-level controllers. Experiments on multiple locomotion agents show that our framework reduces final-step tracking error by 94.75% -- 99.79% compared with recent state-of-the-art baselines and achieves comparable deployment performance with about 23.8% of their interaction data.

</details>


### [106] [Your AI Travel Agent Would Book You a Bullfight: An Agentic Benchmark for Implicit Animal Welfare in Frontier AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18142)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Oliver Tulio,Joel Christoph,Miles Tidmarsh,Carol Kline,Arturs Kanepajs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TAC是首个衡量AI智能体在代理部署中避免动物剥削行为的基准测试。在12个旅行预订场景（扩展至48个样本）中，7个前沿模型均低于随机水平（64%），最佳模型（Claude Opus 4.7）仅达53%。系统提示中加入一句福利意识语句可显著提升Claude和GPT-5.5的性能（47-63个百分点），但对DeepSeek和Gemini提升有限。审计结果表明低于随机水平并非源于模型识别出评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI与动物福利基准仅评估模型对问答提示的文本回应，未能检验这些福利推理能力是否能迁移到需要实际操作工具的代理部署场景中。

Method: 构建TAC基准，包含12个手工编写的旅行预订场景覆盖6类动物剥削类别，扩展至48个样本以控制价格、评分和位置等混淆变量。评估7个前沿模型，并使用Inspect Scout审计288个基础条件转录本。

Result: 所有模型均低于64%的随机水平；最佳模型Claude Opus 4.7仅53%；福利意识提示在Claude和GPT-5.5上提升47-63个百分点，但在DeepSeek和Gemini上提升不足12个百分点；审计未发现评估意识迹象。

Conclusion: 当前AI智能体在避免动物剥削方面表现不佳，文本回应福利基准无法迁移到代理行动，提示工程效果因模型而异，需关注类别层面的文化差异及欧盟AI实践规范中的系统性风险框架。

Abstract: AI agents are moving from advisors to actors, booking travel, planning menus, and running procurement on behalf of users. Existing benchmarks for AI and animal welfare evaluate model text responses to question-answer prompts, leaving open whether the welfare reasoning surfaced in those responses transfers to agentic deployment where the model must take actions with tools. We introduce TAC (Travel Agent Compassion), the first agentic benchmark measuring whether AI agents avoid options involving animal exploitation when acting on behalf of users. TAC presents an AI agent with twelve hand-authored travel booking scenarios across six categories of animal exploitation, augmented to forty-eight samples to control for price, rating, and position confounds. We evaluate seven frontier models from four labs. Every model scores below the chance level of sixty-four percent, with the best performer (Claude Opus 4.7) at fifty-three percent. A single welfare-aware sentence in the system prompt yields gains of forty-seven to sixty-three percentage points in Claude and GPT-5.5, twenty-six points in GPT-5.2, and under twelve points in DeepSeek and Gemini. An auxiliary Inspect Scout audit of 288 base-condition transcripts from the top two performers, using Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite as judge, flags zero transcripts for evaluation awareness, suggesting the below-chance rates do not stem from the models recognising the evaluation. We discuss implications for category-level variation across cultural domains, the limits of text-response welfare benchmarks, and the EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice systemic risk framework.

</details>


### [107] [Memory as a Wasting Asset: Pricing Flash Endurance for Embodied Agents, and the Limits of Doing So](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18144)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将机器人闪存耐久度视为折旧资本，通过耐久度影子价格η构建RAM/片上NVM/云存储的分层成本优化模型。价值-写入关联χ的符号决定最优存储策略：χ>0时最优策略非单调，最有价值数据应移出闪存。实测表明χ的符号取决于部署场景（正/零/负），耐久度约束在廉价边缘机器人上显著，但磨损感知控制对任务价值无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人闪存写入耐久度（P/E周期）是不可再生资源，但现有机器人存储系统未对每次写入进行价值定价，导致存储策略可能非成本最优。

Method: 提出耐久度影子价格η，构建基于磨损增强的每字节成本索引，在RAM/片上NVM/云三层层级中进行成本最小化数据放置。通过价值-写入关联χ分析最优策略的单调性，并在真实机器人日志上测量χ的符号。

Result: χ的符号取决于部署场景：长期重复操作中χ>0（约+1.0×10⁻³），短期任务中χ≈0，非重复遥操作中χ<0。耐久度约束在3000 P/E的TLC上不显著，但在1000 P/E的QLC/eMMC上起关键作用。磨损感知控制器与基于任务价值的路由策略性能相同。

Conclusion: 磨损感知的数据放置优化了设备寿命和成本，但对任务价值无显著提升。非单调最优策略在理论上成立但尚未被实验观测到，价值-写入关联χ的度量依赖于价值代理，结果存在边界条件。

Abstract: A robot's flash endurance is a non-renewable stock: every persisted write spends one of a few thousand program/erase cycles and never refills, yet no fielded robot memory system prices which memories are worth an erase cycle. We treat embodied memory as depreciating capital and price that stock with a single endurance shadow price $η$, which makes cost-minimizing placement across a RAM / on-board NVM / cloud hierarchy a threshold in a wear-augmented per-byte index. The index is cost-optimal whatever the sign of the value-write association $χ$; only when $χ> 0$ does the optimum turn non-monotone, sending a robot's most valuable memories off its flash.
  The pivot is thus empirical, and we measure $χ$ on real robot logs at a pre-specified gate: its sign is a property of the deployment regime -- positive on recurrent long-horizon manipulation ($\hatχ \approx +1.0 \times 10^{-3}$, replicated at full power), null on a shorter-horizon suite, and negative on non-recurrent teleoperation. Two boundaries scope the result. The endurance budget is dormant on premium 3,000-P/E TLC at datasheet prices and binding on the commodity QLC/eMMC ($\sim$1,000 P/E) that cheaper edge robots run. And where it binds, a learned wear-aware controller only ties price-based routing on task value, because realized value is tier-invariant across RAM, NVM, and cloud: the rent governs device lifetime and cost, not task performance. Whether wear-aware placement improves task value remains open -- $χ$ is measured against a value proxy, and the non-monotone optimum, while proven, is not yet observed in data.

</details>


### [108] [WEQA: Wearable hEalth Question Answering with Query-Adaptive Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18147)
*Yuwei Zhang,Tong Xia,Bianca Emmerich,Yu Yvonne Wu,Dimitris Spathis,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WEQA框架，通过查询自适应代理架构将LLM推理与可穿戴分析工具结合，在可穿戴健康数据问答任务上相比基线提升24%准确率，并在专家评估中展现出更高的实用性和临床合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器产生的连续、高维、纵向数据难以与LLM预训练的文本分布对齐，且传感器模态多样性和用户意图多样性无法被固定推理流程或单一预训练基础模型有效处理。

Method: 提出WEQA框架，采用LLM控制器合成执行计划，动态将查询路由至合适的传感器分析和预训练模型组合，并结合外部知识进行基于证据的响应审核。同时构建涵盖四个公开可穿戴数据集的基准，包括三个健康领域的分析和预测任务。

Result: 框架比LLM和基于agent的基线准确率高24%；在12名医学专家和8名用户的盲审研究中，实用性和临床合理性均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的代理框架能够有效统一LLM推理与可穿戴数据分析工具，解决可穿戴健康数据问答中模态对齐和意图多样性的核心挑战。

Abstract: Language models are remarkably capable at medical question answering, in some cases surpassing the accuracy of general physicians. However, answering questions about wearable health data remains challenging and understudied, as these ubiquitous sensors produce continuous, high-dimensional, and longitudinal data, which is non-trivial to align with text-centric distributions in LLM pretraining. The diversity of sensor modalities and user intents cannot be effectively handled by a fixed reasoning workflow or a single pretrained foundation model. To address these challenges, we propose WEQA, a query-adaptive agent framework that unifies LLM reasoning with specialized wearable analytical and modeling tools. An LLM controller is employed to synthesize execution plans and dynamically route each query to the appropriate combination of sensor analysis and pretrained models, and perform grounded response auditing with external knowledge. We also curate a benchmark spanning four open wearable datasets comprising analytic and predictive tasks in three different health domains. Experiments show that our framework is 24% more accurate than LLM and agentic baselines, and a blinded study with 12 medical experts and 8 users shows substantial gains in usefulness and clinical soundness.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Cardiac Electrophysiology Digital Twins Through Agentic Discovery of Hybrid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18154)
*Ziqi Zhou,Yubo Ye,Sumeet Atul Vadhavka,Linwei Wang,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEADS框架，利用LLM代理在结构化动作空间中自动发现心脏电生理混合模型，结合物理先验与梯度下降参数拟合，在合成和真实数据上优于人工设计和纯LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖专家手动设计混合物理-神经网络架构，需要深厚领域知识且无法跨患者迁移；现有LLM方法虽泛化能力强但缺乏心脏模拟所需的结构先验，导致数值不稳定。

Method: 将心脏EP领域知识形式化为结构化动作空间，LLM代理通过迭代推理-行动循环选择、组合和优化混合模型，同时梯度下降负责参数拟合，确保每个候选模型物理可解释、数值稳定且支持开放架构发现。

Result: 在三种反应模型的合成数据和真实心脏EP数据上，LEADS均优于人工设计的混合模型和其他基于LLM的混合建模方法。

Conclusion: LEADS通过结构化先验约束LLM的探索空间，在保持自动发现能力的同时保证了心脏模拟的物理一致性和数值稳定性，为个性化心脏数字孪生构建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Building personalized cardiac electrophysiology (EP) digital twins requires identifying the appropriate model structure for each patient, not merely fitting parameters. Traditional methods rely on experts to manually prescribe hybrid physics-neural architectures, which requires deep domain expertise and does not transfer across patients. Recent works have applied large language models (LLMs) to generate or act as hybrid models. However, despite their promising generalization capacity, these LLM-based methods lack the structural priors needed for stable cardiac simulations. Hence, we propose LEADS, a framework that formulates cardiac EP domain knowledge as a structured action space and utilizes an LLM agent to discover hybrid models. The agent follows an iterative reasoning-and-action loop to select, combine, and refine hybrid models, whilst gradient descent handles parameter fitting. The proposed LEADS designs every candidate model towards physically grounded, interpretable, and numerically stable, while allowing open-ended architectural discovery. We validate LEADS on synthetic data with three ground-truth reaction models and on real cardiac EP data, demonstrating that it outperforms both human-designed hybrid models and other LLM-based hybrid modeling.

</details>


### [110] [DRFLOW: A Deep Research Benchmark for Personalized Workflow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18191)
*Md Tawkat Islam Khondaker,Raymond Li,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Issam H. Laradji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRFLOW是一个用于评估深度研究系统预测个性化工作流程能力的基准，包含100个跨5个领域的任务、1,246个参考步骤和3,900多个来源，并提出DRFLOW-Agent作为参考代理，实验表明虽然DRFA相比强基线提升了最高10.02%的F1分数，但仍有很大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统主要关注生成报告和摘要，但企业任务更需要代理识别具体的可执行工作流程（即动作步骤序列），例如"如何在固定预算下请求新员工"而非简单总结政策，因此需要评估代理从异构来源预测个性化工作流程的能力。

Method: 构建了DRFLOW基准，包含100个跨5个领域的任务，1,246个参考工作流程步骤，基于3,900多个来源；定义了7项诊断指标（事实基础、步骤恢复、结构排序、条件解析、个性化）；提出了DRFLOW-Agent（DRFA）作为面向工作流程的参考代理。

Result: DRFA在平均F1分数上相比强基线代理提升了最高10.02%，但在工作流程相关指标上仍有显著改进空间。

Conclusion: 预测完整且正确的个性化工作流程仍是深度研究领域一个具有挑战性的前沿问题。

Abstract: Deep research (DR) systems are increasingly used for complex information-seeking tasks, but existing works mainly focus on generating reports and summaries. In contrast, many enterprise tasks instead require an agent to identify concrete workflows which is a sequence of action-steps. For example, rather than summarizing budgeting policies, an agent should be able to determine the steps needed to answer a question such as: "How do I request new headcount given a fixed budget?". Therefore, we introduce DRFLOW, a benchmark for evaluating personalized workflows predicted by agents from heterogeneous sources. Each task requires the agent to identify relevant evidence from scattered sources, then use that evidence to predict the correct action-step sequence for the user's task. DRFLOW contains 100 tasks across five domains, with 1,246 reference workflow steps grounded in more than 3,900 sources.
